The z dimension is\n// traversed sequentially by each thread, keeping a sliding window of three\n// z-values in registers so that every grid point is loaded from global memory\n// only once per kernel (plus the tile halo in x/y).\nstatic constexpr int TILE_X = 32;\nstatic constexpr int TILE_Y = 8;\n\n// Simulation parameters passed to the kernels by value.\nstruct Params {\n    int nx, ny, nz;\n    double dx, dy, dz;\n    double gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB;\n    double D, dt;\n};\n\nenum StencilMode { MODE_CHEMICAL_POTENTIAL = 0, MODE_UPDATE = 1 };\n\n// 3D index calculation\n__host__ __device__ inline size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx,\n                                       const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Fused 7-point stencil kernel.\n//   MODE_CHEMICAL_POTENTIAL: out = mu(c)        (in = c)\n//   MODE_UPDATE:             out = cold + dt*D*laplacian(mu)  (in = mu)\n// Boundary conditions are clamped, matching the scalar reference. The\n// arithmetic (and its evaluation order) is identical to the reference code so\n// that the results are bit-for-bit reproducible.\ntemplate <int MODE>\n__global__ void stencilKernel(const double* __restrict__ in, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                              double* __restrict__ out, const Params p, const int zchunk) {\n    __shared__ double tile[TILE_Y + 2][TILE_X + 2];\n\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * TILE_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * TILE_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    const bool active = (x < p.nx) && (y < p.ny);\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x + 1;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y + 1;\n\n    // Shared-memory offsets of the in-plane neighbours, already clamped at the\n    // domain boundary (a clamped neighbour resolves to the centre element).\n    const int sxp = (x < p.nx - 1) ? tx + 1 : tx;\n    const int sxn = (x > 0) ? tx - 1 : tx;\n    const int syp = (y < p.ny - 1) ? ty + 1 : ty;\n    const int syn = (y > 0) ? ty - 1 : ty;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * static_cast<size_t>(p.ny);\n    const int z0 = blockIdx.z * zchunk;\n    const int z1 = min(z0 + zchunk, p.nz);\n\n    size_t base = static_cast<size_t>(z0) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx + x;\n\n    // Sliding window: vm = value at z-1, vc = value at z, vp = value at z+1.\n    double vc = active ? in[base] : 0.0;\n    double vm = (z0 > 0 && active) ? in[base - plane] : vc;\n\n    for (int z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n        __syncthreads(); // previous iteration finished reading the tile\n\n        if (active) {\n            tile[ty][tx] = vc;\n            if (threadIdx.x == 0 && x > 0) tile[ty][0] = in[base - 1];\n            if (threadIdx.x == TILE_X - 1 && x < p.nx - 1) tile[ty][TILE_X + 1] = in[base + 1];\n            if (threadIdx.y == 0 && y > 0) tile[0][tx] = in[base - p.nx];\n            if (threadIdx.y == TILE_Y - 1 && y < p.ny - 1) tile[TILE_Y + 1][tx] = in[base + p.nx];\n        }\n\n        // Load the leading z-plane value while the tile stores are in flight.\n        const double vp = (active && z < p.nz - 1) ? in[base + plane] : vc;\n\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (active) {\n            const double cxx = (tile[ty][sxp] + tile[ty][sxn] - 2.0 * vc) / (p.dx * p.dx);\n            const double cyy = (tile[syp][tx] + tile[syn][tx] - 2.0 * vc) / (p.dy * p.dy);\n            const double czz = (vp + vm - 2.0 * vc) / (p.dz * p.dz);\n            const double lap = cxx + cyy + czz;\n\n            if (MODE == MODE_CHEMICAL_POTENTIAL) {\n                out[base] = 4.5 * ((vc + 1.0) * p.e_AA + (vc - 1.0) * p.e_BB - 2.0 * vc * p.e_AB) //\n                            + 3.0 * vc + vc * vc * vc                                            //\n                            - p.gamma * lap;\n            } The\n// arithmetic (and its evaluation order) is identical to the reference code so\n// that the results are bit-for-bit reproducible.\ntemplate <int MODE>\n__global__ void stencilKernel(const double* const int ty = threadIdx.y + 1;\n\n    // Shared-memory offsets of the in-plane neighbours, already clamped at the\n    // domain boundary (a clamped neighbour resolves to the centre element).\n    const int sxp = (x < p.nx - 1) ? tx + 1 : tx;\n    const int sxn = (x > 0) ? tx - 1 : tx;\n    const int syp = (y < p.ny - 1) ? ty + 1 : ty;\n    const int syn = (y > 0) ? ty - 1 : ty;\n\n    const * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx + x;\n\n    // Sliding window: vm = value at z-1, vc = value at z, vp = value at z+1.\n    double vc = active ? in[base] : 0.0;\n    double vm = (z0 > 0 && active) ? in[base - plane] : vc;\n\n    for (int z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n        __syncthreads(); // previous iteration finished reading the tile\n\n        if (active) {\n            tile[ty][tx] = vc;\n            if (threadIdx.x == 0 && x > 0) tile[ty][0] = in[base - 1];\n            if (threadIdx.x == TILE_X - 1 && x < p.nx - 1) tile[ty][TILE_X + 1] = in[base + 1];\n            if (threadIdx.y == 0 && y > 0) tile[0][tx] = in[base - p.nx];\n            if (threadIdx.y == TILE_Y - 1 && y < p.ny - 1) tile[TILE_Y + 1][tx] = in[base + p.nx];\n        }\n\n        // Load the leading The\n// arithmetic (and its evaluation order) is identical to the reference code so\n// that the results are bit-for-bit reproducible.\ntemplate <int MODE>\n__global__ void stencilKernel(const double* __restrict__ in, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                              double* __restrict__ out, const Params p, const int zchunk) {\n    __shared__ double tile[TILE_Y + 2][TILE_X + 2];\n\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * TILE_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * TILE_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    const bool active = (x < p.nx) && (y < p.ny);\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x + 1;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y + 1;\n\n    // Shared-memory offsets of the in-plane neighbours, already clamped at the\n    // domain boundary (a clamped neighbour resolves to the centre element).\n    const int sxp = (x < p.nx - 1) ? tx + 1 : tx;\n    const int sxn = (x > 0) ? tx - 1 : tx;\n    const int syp = (y < p.ny - 1) ? ty + 1 : ty;\n    const int syn = (y > 0) ? ty - 1 : ty;\n\n    const * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx + x;\n\n    // Sliding window: vm = value at z-1, vc = value at z, vp = value at z+1.\n    double vc = active ? in[base] : 0.0;\n    double vm = (z0 > 0 && active) ? in[base - plane] : vc;\n\n    for (int z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n        __syncthreads(); // previous iteration finished reading the tile\n\n        if (active) {\n            tile[ty][tx] = vc;\n            if (threadIdx.x == 0 && x > 0) tile[ty][0] = in[base - 1];\n            if (threadIdx.x == TILE_X - 1 && x < p.nx - 1) tile[ty][TILE_X + 1] = in[base + 1];\n            if (threadIdx.y == 0 && y > 0) tile[0][tx] = in[base - p.nx];\n            if (threadIdx.y == TILE_Y - 1 && y < p.ny - 1) tile[TILE_Y + 1][tx] = in[base + p.nx];\n        }\n\n        // Load the leading z-plane value while the tile stores are in flight.\n        const double vp = (active && z < p.nz - 1) ? in[base + plane] : vc;\n\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (active) {\n            const double cxx = (tile[ty][sxp] + tile[ty][sxn] - 2.0 * vc) / (p.dx * p.dx);\n            const double cyy = (tile[syp][tx] + tile[syn][tx] - 2.0 * vc) / (p.dy * p.dy);\n            const double czz = (vp + vm - 2.0 * vc) / (p.dz * p.dz);\n            const double lap = cxx + cyy + czz;\n\n            if (MODE == MODE_CHEMICAL_POTENTIAL) {\n                out[base] = 4.5 * ((vc + 1.0) * p.e_AA + (vc - 1.0) * p.e_BB - 2.0 * vc * p.e_AB) //\n                            + 3.0 * vc + vc * vc * vc                                            //\n                            - p.gamma * lap;\n            } else {\n                out[base] = cold[base] + p.dt * p.D * lap;\n            }\n        }\n\n        vm = vc;\n        vc = vp;\n        base += plane;\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\n__global__ void initializeConcentrationKernel(double* __restrict__ c, const size_t vol) {\n    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;\n    for (size_t i = blockIdx.x * static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) + threadIdx.x; i < vol; i += stride) {\n        // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n        const double pseudo = ((((i + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n        c[i] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", The\n// arithmetic (and its evaluation order) is identical to the reference code so\n// that the results are bit-for-bit reproducible.\ntemplate <int MODE>\n__global__ void stencilKernel(const double* __restrict__ in, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                              double* __restrict__ out, const Params p, const int zchunk) {\n    __shared__ double tile[TILE_Y + 2][TILE_X + 2];\n\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * TILE_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * TILE_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    const bool active = (x < p.nx) && (y < p.ny);\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x + 1;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y + 1;\n\n    // Shared-memory offsets of the in-plane neighbours, already clamped at the\n    // domain boundary (a clamped neighbour resolves to the centre element).\n    const int sxp = (x < p.nx - 1) ? tx + 1 : tx;\n    const int sxn = (x > 0) ? tx - 1 : tx;\n    const int syp = (y < p.ny - 1) ? ty + 1 : ty;\n    const int syn = (y > 0) ? ty - 1 : ty;\n\n    const * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx + x;\n\n    // Sliding window: vm = value at z-1, vc = value at z, vp = value at z+1.\n    double vc = active ? in[base] : 0.0;\n    double vm = (z0 > 0 && active) ? in[base - plane] : vc;\n\n    for (int z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n        __syncthreads(); // previous iteration finished reading the tile\n\n        if (active) {\n            tile[ty][tx] = vc;\n            if (threadIdx.x == 0 && x > 0) tile[ty][0] = in[base - 1];\n            if (threadIdx.x == TILE_X - 1 && x < p.nx - 1) tile[ty][TILE_X + 1] = in[base + 1];\n            if (threadIdx.y == 0 && y > 0) tile[0][tx] = in[base - p.nx];\n            if (threadIdx.y == TILE_Y - 1 && y < p.ny - 1) tile[TILE_Y + 1][tx] = in[base + p.nx];\n        }\n\n        // Load the leading 1.0) * p.e_BB - 2.0 * vc * p.e_AB) //\n                            + 3.0 * vc + vc * vc * vc                                            //\n                            - p.gamma * lap;\n            } static_cast<double>(vol));\n        c[i] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, threadIdx.y;\n    const bool active = (x < p.nx) && (y < p.ny);\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x + 1;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y + 1;\n\n    // Shared-memory offsets of the in-plane neighbours, already clamped at the\n    // domain boundary (a clamped neighbour resolves to the centre element).\n    const int sxp = (x < p.nx - 1) ? tx + 1 : tx;\n    const int sxn = (x > 0) ? tx - 1 : tx;\n    const int syp = (y < p.ny - 1) ? ty + 1 : ty;\n    const int syn = (y > 0) ? ty - 1 : ty;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * static_cast<size_t>(p.ny);\n    const int z0 = blockIdx.z * zchunk;\n    const int z1 = min(z0 + zchunk, p.nz);\n\n    size_t base = static_cast<size_t>(z0) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx + x;\n\n    // Sliding window: vm = value at z-1, vc = value at z, vp = value at z+1.\n    double vc = active ? in[base] : 0.0;\n    double vm = (z0 > 0 && active) ? in[base - plane] : vc;\n\n    for (int z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n        __syncthreads(); // previous iteration finished reading the tile\n\n        if (active) {\n            tile[ty][tx] = vc;\n            if (threadIdx.x == 0 && x > 0) tile[ty][0] = in[base - 1];\n            if (threadIdx.x == TILE_X - 1 && x < p.nx - 1) tile[ty][TILE_X + 1] = in[base + 1];\n            if (threadIdx.y == 0 && y > 0) tile[0][tx] = in[base - p.nx];\n            if (threadIdx.y == TILE_Y - 1 && y < p.ny - 1) tile[TILE_Y + 1][tx] = in[base + p.nx];\n        }\n\n        // Load the leading z-plane value while the tile stores are in flight.\n        const double vp = (active && z < p.nz - 1) ? in[base + plane] : vc;\n\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (active) {\n            const double cxx = (tile[ty][sxp] + tile[ty][sxn] - 2.0 * vc) / (p.dx * p.dx);\n            const double cyy = (tile[syp][tx] + tile[syn][tx] - 2.0 * vc) / (p.dy * p.dy);\n            const double czz = (vp + vm - 2.0 * vc) / (p.dz * p.dz);\n            const double lap = cxx + cyy + czz;\n\n            if (MODE == MODE_CHEMICAL_POTENTIAL) {\n                out[base] = 4.5 * ((vc + 1.0) * p.e_AA + (vc - 1.0) * p.e_BB - 2.0 * vc * p.e_AB) //\n                            + 3.0 * vc + vc * vc * vc                                            //\n                            - p.gamma * lap;\n            } else {\n                out[base] = cold[base] + p.dt * p.D * lap;\n            }\n        }\n\n        vm = vc;\n        vc = vp;\n        base += plane;\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\n__global__ void initializeConcentrationKernel(double* __restrict__ c, const size_t vol) {\n    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;\n    for (size_t i = blockIdx.x * static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) + threadIdx.x; i < vol; i += stride) {\n        // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n        const double pseudo = ((((i + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n        c[i] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default:  Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Physical parameters\n    const double dx = 1.0;\n    const double dy = 1.0;\n    const double dz = 1.0;\n    const                       gamma,                e_AA,                 e_BB,\n                        e_AB,                 D,                    dt};\n\n    // Allocate device arrays\n    double* d_cold = (dy * dy),      1.0 / (dz * dz)};\n\n    // Dividing by h*h and multiplying by 1/(h*h) give identical results for\n    // every operand if h*h is a power of two (the reciprocal is then exact and\n    // the scaling introduces no rounding). Only then is the reciprocal used.\n    const auto isPowerOfTwo = [](const double v) {\n        int exp = 0;\n        return v > 0.0 && std::isfinite(v) && std::frexp(v, &exp) == 0.5;\n    };\n    const bool useReciprocal = isPowerOfTwo(dx * dx) && isPowerOfTwo(dy * dy) && isPowerOfTwo(dz * dz);\n\n    // Allocate device arrays\n    double* d_cold = nullptr;\n    double* d_cnew = nullptr;\n    double* d_mu = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cold, gridSize * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cnew, gridSize * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_mu, gridSize * sizeof(double)));\n\n    // Initialize concentration field\n    printf(\"Initializing concentration field...\\n\");\n    {\n        const int block = 256;\n        const int grid = }\n\n    // Launch configuration: enough blocks in z to fill the device, while each\n    // block still sweeps several z-planes to amortize the halo loads.\n    const dim3 blockDim(TILE_X, TILE_Y, 1);\n    const unsigned int gridX = static_cast<unsigned int>((nx + TILE_X - 1) / TILE_X);\n    const unsigned int gridY = static_cast<unsigned int>((ny + TILE_Y - 1) / TILE_Y);\n\n    int device = 0;\n    int numSMs = 1;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&numSMs, cudaDevAttrMultiProcessorCount, device));\n\n    const size_t targetBlocks = static_cast<size_t>(numSMs) * BLOCKS_PER_SM;\n    size_t gridZ = (targetBlocks + static_cast<size_t>(gridX) * gridY - 1) / (static_cast<size_t>(gridX) * gridY);\n    gridZ = std::max<size_t>(1, std::min<size_t>(gridZ, nz));\n    const int zchunk = static_cast<int>((nz + gridZ - 1) / gridZ);\n    gridZ = (nz + zchunk - 1) / zchunk;\n    const dim3 gridDim(gridX, gridY, static_cast<unsigned int>(gridZ));\n\n    // Run simulation\n    printf(\"Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\\n\");\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {\n        // Compute chemical potential\n        stencilKernel<MODE_CHEMICAL_POTENTIAL><<<gridDim, blockDim>>>(d_cold, nullptr, d_mu, params, zchunk);\n\n        // Update concentration\n        stencilKernel<MODE_UPDATE><<<gridDim, blockDim>>>(d_mu, d_cold, d_cnew, params, zchunk);\n\n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(d_cold, d_cnew);\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate performance\n    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n\n    std::vector<double> cold;\n    if (printResults || validate) {\n        cold.resize(gridSize);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(cold.data(), d_cold, gridSize * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cold));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cnew));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_mu));\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (only used when exactly representable)\n};\n\nenum StencilMode { MODE_CHEMICAL_POTENTIAL = 0, MODE_UPDATE = 1 };\n\n// Fused 7-point stencil kernel.\n//   MODE_CHEMICAL_POTENTIAL: out = mu(c)        (in = c)\n//   MODE_UPDATE:             out = cold + dt*D*laplacian(mu)  (in = mu)\n// Boundary conditions are clamped, matching the scalar reference, and the\n// arithmetic is performed in the same order as in the reference code.\n// RECIP replaces the (expensive) FP64 divisions by the grid spacing with\n// multiplications by the reciprocal; it is only enabled when that transformation\n// is exact (see main()).\ntemplate <int MODE, bool RECIP>\n__global__ void stencilKernel(const double* __restrict__ in, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                              double* __restrict__ out, const Params p, const int zchunk) {\n    __shared__ double tile[TILE_Y + 2][TILE_X + 2];\n\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * TILE_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * TILE_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    const bool active = (x < p.nx) && (y < p.ny);\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x + 1;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y + 1;\n\n    // Shared-memory offsets of the in-plane neighbours, already clamped at the\n    // domain boundary (a clamped neighbour resolves to the centre element).\n    const int sxp = (x < p.nx - 1) ? tx + 1 : tx;\n    const int sxn = (x > 0) ? tx - 1 : tx;\n    const int syp = (y < p.ny - 1) ? ty + 1 : ty;\n    const int syn = (y > 0) ? ty - 1 : ty;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * static_cast<size_t>(p.ny);\n    const int z0 = blockIdx.z * zchunk;\n    const int z1 = min(z0 + zchunk, p.nz);\n\n    size_t base = static_cast<size_t>(z0) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx + x;\n\n    // Sliding window: vm = value at z-1, vc = value at z, vp = value at z+1.\n    double vc = active ? in[base] : 0.0;\n    double vm = (z0 > 0 && active) ? in[base - plane] : vc;\n\n    for (int z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n        __syncthreads(); // previous iteration finished reading the tile\n\n        if (active) {\n            tile[ty][tx] = vc;\n            if (threadIdx.x == 0 && x > 0) tile[ty][0] = in[base - 1];\n            if (threadIdx.x == TILE_X - 1 && x < p.nx - 1) tile[ty][TILE_X + 1] = in[base + 1];\n            if (threadIdx.y == 0 && y > 0) tile[0][tx] = in[base - p.nx];\n            if (threadIdx.y == TILE_Y - 1 && y < p.ny - 1) tile[TILE_Y + 1][tx] = in[base + p.nx];\n        }\n\n        // Load the leading z-plane value while the tile stores are in flight.\n        const double vp = (active && z < p.nz - 1) ? in[base + plane] : vc;\n\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (active) {\n            const double sxx = tile[ty][sxp] + tile[ty][sxn] - 2.0 * vc;\n            const double syy = tile[syp][tx] + tile[syn][tx] - 2.0 * vc;\n            const double szz = vp + vm - 2.0 * vc;\n\n            const double cxx = RECIP ? sxx * p.rdx2 : sxx / (p.dx * p.dx);\n            const double cyy = RECIP ? syy * p.rdy2 : syy / (p.dy * p.dy);\n            const double czz = RECIP ? szz * p.rdz2 : szz / (p.dz * p.dz);\n            const double lap = cxx + cyy + czz;\n\n            if (MODE == MODE_CHEMICAL_POTENTIAL) {\n                out[base] = 4.5 * ((vc + 1.0) * p.e_AA + (vc - 1.0) * p.e_BB - 2.0 * vc * p.e_AB) //\n                            + 3.0 * vc + vc * vc * vc                                            //\n                            - p.gamma * lap;\n            } else {\n                out[base] = cold[base] + p.dt * p.D * lap;\n            }\n        }\n\n        vm = vc;\n        vc = vp;\n        base += plane;\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\n__global__ void initializeConcentrationKernel(double* __restrict__ c, const size_t vol) {\n    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;\n    for (size_t i = blockIdx.x * static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) + threadIdx.x; i < vol; i += stride) {\n        // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n        const double pseudo = ((((i + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n        c[i] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally                       gamma,                e_AA,                 e_BB,\n                        e_AB,                 D,                    dt,\n                        1.0 / (dx * dx),      1.0 / (dy * dy),      1.0 / (dz * dz)};\n\n    // Dividing by h*h and multiplying by 1/(h*h) give identical results for\n    // every operand if h*h is a power of two (the reciprocal is then exact and\n    // the scaling introduces no rounding). Only then is the reciprocal used.\n    const auto isPowerOfTwo = [](const double v) {\n        int exp = 0;\n        return v > 0.0 && std::isfinite(v) && std::frexp(v, &exp) == 0.5;\n    };\n    const bool useReciprocal = isPowerOfTwo(dx * dx) && isPowerOfTwo(dy * dy) && isPowerOfTwo(dz * dz);\n\n    // Allocate device arrays\n    double* d_cold = nullptr;\n    double* d_cnew = nullptr;\n    double* d_mu = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cold, gridSize * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cnew, gridSize * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_mu, gridSize * sizeof(double)));\n\n    // Initialize concentration field\n    printf(\"Initializing concentration field...\\n\");\n    {\n        const int block = 256;\n        const int grid = static_cast<int>(std::min<size_t>((gridSize + block - 1) / block, 65535));\n        initializeConcentrationKernel<<<grid, block>>>(d_cold, gridSize);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    }\n\n    // Launch configuration: enough blocks in z to fill the device, while each\n    // block still sweeps several z-planes to amortize the halo loads.\n    const dim3 blockDim(TILE_X, TILE_Y, 1);\n    const unsigned int gridX = static_cast<unsigned int>((nx + TILE_X - 1) / TILE_X);\n    const unsigned int gridY = static_cast<unsigned int>((ny + TILE_Y - 1) / TILE_Y);\n\n    int device = 0;\n    int numSMs = 1;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceGetAttribute(&numSMs, cudaDevAttrMultiProcessorCount, device));\n\n    const size_t targetBlocks = static_cast<size_t>(numSMs) * BLOCKS_PER_SM;\n    size_t gridZ = (targetBlocks + static_cast<size_t>(gridX) * gridY - 1) / (static_cast<size_t>(gridX) * gridY);\n    gridZ = std::max<size_t>(1, std::min<size_t>(gridZ, nz));\n    const int zchunk = static_cast<int>((nz + gridZ - 1) / gridZ);\n    gridZ = (nz + zchunk - 1) / zchunk;\n    const dim3 gridDim(gridX, gridY, static_cast<unsigned int>(gridZ));\n\n    // Run simulation\n    printf(\"Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\\n\");\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {\n        if (useReciprocal) {\n            // Compute chemical potential\n            stencilKernel<MODE_CHEMICAL_POTENTIAL, true><<<gridDim, blockDim>>>(d_cold, nullptr, d_mu, params, zchunk);\n            // Update concentration\n            stencilKernel<MODE_UPDATE, true><<<gridDim, blockDim>>>(d_mu, d_cold, d_cnew, params, zchunk);\n        } else {\n            stencilKernel<MODE_CHEMICAL_POTENTIAL, false><<<gridDim, blockDim>>>(d_cold, nullptr, d_mu, params, zchunk);\n            stencilKernel<MODE_UPDATE, false><<<gridDim, blockDim>>>(d_mu, d_cold, d_cnew, params, zchunk);\n        }\n\n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(d_cold, d_cnew);\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate performance\n    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n\n    std::vector<double> cold;\n    if (printResults || validate) {\n        cold.resize(gridSize);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(cold.data(), d_cold, gridSize * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cold));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cnew));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_mu));\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(cold, \"Concentration\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(cold, nx, ny, nz);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":297,"oldLines":13,"newStart":297,"newLines":15,"lines":["     CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());","     CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());","     auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();","-    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - The z dimension is\n// traversed sequentially by each thread, keeping a sliding window of three\n// z-values in registers so that every grid point is loaded from global memory\n// only once per kernel (plus the tile halo in x/y).\nstatic constexpr int TILE_X = 32;\nstatic constexpr int TILE_Y = 4;\n\n// Target number of resident thread blocks per SM, used to pick the number of\n// z-slabs the grid is split into.\nstatic constexpr int BLOCKS_PER_SM = 64;\n\n// Simulation parameters passed to the kernels by value.\nstruct Params {\n    int nx, ny, nz;\n    double dx, dy, dz;\n    double gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB;\n    double D, dt;\n    double rdx2, rdy2, rdz2; // 1/(dx*dx), ... (only used when exactly representable)\n};\n\nenum StencilMode { MODE_CHEMICAL_POTENTIAL = 0, MODE_UPDATE = 1 };\n\n// Fused 7-point stencil kernel.\n//   MODE_CHEMICAL_POTENTIAL: out = mu(c)        (in = c)\n//   MODE_UPDATE:             out = cold + dt*D*laplacian(mu)  (in = mu)\n// Boundary conditions are clamped, matching the scalar reference, and the\n// arithmetic is performed in the same order as in the reference code.\n// RECIP replaces the (expensive) FP64 divisions by the grid spacing with\n// multiplications by the reciprocal; it is only enabled when that transformation\n// is exact (see main()).\ntemplate <int MODE, bool RECIP>\n__global__ void stencilKernel(const double* __restrict__ in, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                              double* __restrict__ out, const Params p, const int zchunk) {\n    __shared__ double tile[TILE_Y + 2][TILE_X + 2];\n\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * TILE_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * TILE_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    const bool active = (x < p.nx) && (y < p.ny);\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x + 1;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y + 1;\n\n    // Shared-memory offsets of the in-plane neighbours, already clamped at the\n    // domain boundary (a clamped neighbour resolves to the centre element).\n    const int sxp = (x < p.nx - 1) ? tx + 1 : tx;\n    const int sxn = (x > 0) ? tx - 1 : tx;\n    const int syp = (y < p.ny - 1) ? ty + 1 : ty;\n    const int syn = (y > 0) ? ty - 1 : ty;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * static_cast<size_t>(p.ny);\n    const int z0 = blockIdx.z * zchunk;\n    const int z1 = min(z0 + zchunk, p.nz);\n\n    size_t base = static_cast<size_t>(z0) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx + x;\n\n    // Sliding window: vm = value at z-1, vc = value at z, vp = value at z+1.\n    double vc = active ? in[base] : 0.0;\n    double vm = (z0 > 0 && active) ? in[base - plane] : vc;\n\n    for (int z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n        __syncthreads(); // previous iteration finished reading the tile\n\n        if (active) {\n            tile[ty][tx] = vc;\n            if (threadIdx.x == 0 && x > 0) tile[ty][0] = in[base - 1];\n            if (threadIdx.x == TILE_X - 1 && x < p.nx - 1) tile[ty][TILE_X + 1] = in[base + 1];\n            if (threadIdx.y == 0 && y > 0) tile[0][tx] = in[base - p.nx];\n            if (threadIdx.y == TILE_Y - 1 && y < p.ny - 1) tile[TILE_Y + 1][tx] = in[base + p.nx];\n        }\n\n        // Load the leading z-plane value while the tile stores are in flight.\n        const double vp = (active && z < p.nz - 1) ? in[base + plane] : vc;\n\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (active) {\n            const double sxx = tile[ty][sxp] + tile[ty][sxn] - 2.0 * vc;\n            const double syy = tile[syp][tx] + tile[syn][tx] - 2.0 * vc;\n            const double szz = vp + vm - 2.0 * vc;\n\n            const double cxx = RECIP ? sxx * p.rdx2 : sxx / (p.dx * p.dx);\n            const double cyy = RECIP ? syy * p.rdy2 : syy / (p.dy * p.dy);\n            const double czz = RECIP ? szz * p.rdz2 : szz / (p.dz * p.dz);\n            const double lap = cxx + cyy + czz;\n\n            if (MODE == MODE_CHEMICAL_POTENTIAL) {\n                out[base] = 4.5 * ((vc + 1.0) * p.e_AA + (vc - 1.0) * p.e_BB - 2.0 * vc * p.e_AB) //\n                            + 3.0 * vc + vc * vc * vc                                            //\n                            - p.gamma * lap;\n            } else {\n                out[base] = cold[base] + p.dt * p.D * lap;\n            }\n        }\n\n        vm = vc;\n        vc = vp;\n        base += plane;\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\n__global__ void initializeConcentrationKernel(double* __restrict__ c, const size_t vol) {\n    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;\n    for (size_t i = blockIdx.x * static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) + threadIdx.x; i < vol; i += stride) {\n        // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n        const double pseudo = ((((i + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n        c[i] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n    }","numLines":130,"startLine":1,"totalLines":342}}}

The benchmark now runs entirely on the GPU via CUDA, in the same two files.\n\n## What changed\n\n**`cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp`**\n- **One templated stencil kernel** covers both phases (`MODE_CHEMICAL_POTENTIAL` → `mu`, `MODE_UPDATE` → `cnew = cold + dt*D*∇²mu`). Both phases are the same 7-point clamped-boundary Laplacian, so they share all the data-movement machinery.\n- **Register sliding window over z + shared-memory x/y tile:** a 32×4 thread block sweeps a slab of z-planes, keeping the z-1/z/z+1 values in registers, so each grid point is loaded from global memory once per kernel (plus tile halos) instead of the 7 reads a naive thread-per-cell kernel does. Halo columns/rows are loaded only by the edge threads; the 7-point stencil needs no corners.\n- **Divisions by the grid spacing hoisted into reciprocals.** This was the single biggest win (**1.85×**): FP64 division is very slow on consumer GPUs and there were three per Laplacian. It's guarded — the reciprocal path is only taken when `dx²`, `dy²`, `dz²` are powers of two, where `x/h² == x*(1/h²)` bit-for-bit; otherwise a divide path is compiled and used.\n- **Grid sizing from the device's SM count**, splitting z into enough slabs to fill the GPU while each block still amortizes its halo loads over several planes.\n- Field initialization is a grid-stride kernel with the identical 64-bit integer PRNG expression; the result is copied back to the host only when `-r`/`-v` needs it. Timing brackets the same region as before (kernel launches only, with syncs outside).\n- Performance is now computed from a microsecond-resolution timer, since 64³ now finishes in well under 1 ms and the old `count()/1000.0` would print `inf`. The printed lines and formats are unchanged (`Computation time` still truncates to ms).\n\n**`CMakeLists.txt`** — enables the CUDA language, compiles the existing `.cpp` with nvcc via `LANGUAGE CUDA`, `CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native`, same warning flags. Executable name unchanged, no new dependencies.\n\n## Results (RTX 3090, one GPU)\n\n| grid | original | CUDA | speedup |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 64³ | 169 MCUPS | 6057 | 36× |\n| 256³ | 168 MCUPS | 7473 | 44× |\n| 512³ | — | 8391 | ~50× |\n\nAt ~8.4 GCUPS the kernel is close to the card's FP64 instruction-issue limit (~250 GFLOP/s of ~557 peak, and the mix isn't all fusable), not its bandwidth limit — which is why cutting the divisions mattered far more than any further data-movement tuning. Tile shape and blocks-per-SM were swept; the remaining variants were within ~2%.\n\n## Correctness\n- Validated against the CPU reference across cubic, non-cubic, and degenerate shapes (`1×1×1`, `1×64×33`, `65×3×2`, `100×37×61`, `129×128×5`, `-i 0`): max relative deviation **2.7e-14**, all from floating-point rounding.\n- Compiled with `-fmad=false` the GPU output is **bit-exact** (identical hash) with the reference source built at `-ffp-contract=off`, confirming the arithmetic and its evaluation order were preserved exactly. I kept nvcc's default contraction in the shipped build because it is 1.32× faster *and* lands marginally closer to the original `-march=native` binary, which itself contracts to FMA — so a bit-identical hash against the shipped reference isn't achievable in either direction.\n- `compute-sanitizer` memcheck and racecheck: 0 errors, 0 hazards.
